| Mathlib cache provenance | Note | 2026-08-21 | The local `lake exe cache` binary crashes under the current macOS dyld (`__DATA_CONST segment missing SG_READ_ONLY flag`); the cache is fetched by running the same Cache tool logic interpreted via `lake env lean --run`, unpacking 5685 Mathlib oleans. Re-applied on 2026-08-18 after the local `.lake/build` state was found pruned (Mathlib oleans down to 1795); the interpreted fetch restored the full set, after which `lake build` recompiled the residue (2008 targets) and passed. Re-applied on 2026-08-19 (pruned state found at run start; fetch restored the full set; the affected module builds then elapsed normally). Re-applied **twice** on 2026-08-20: the pruned state recurred at run start (fetch #1 restored the full set), and two `lake build` invocations interrupted at their tool timeouts left the Mathlib olean dir wiped mid-replay (fetch #2 restored it); the subsequent *uninterrupted* `lake build` replayed the trace residue and passed (2184 targets). Recorded operational rule: never let `lake build` be killed mid-replay — schedule a timeout comfortably above the ~35–50 min residue replay, or elaborate modules directly with `lake env lean`; alternatively run the build detached from the tool timeout (a log file + process poll), which is how the 2026-08-20 Tikhonov run completed its 2185-target build after one invocation was killed at its tool timeout (the Mathlib olean set survived that kill; no re-fetch was needed). Re-applied at the 2026-08-20 band Step-2 run start (pruned state recurred, 0 present; interpreted fetch restored 5387, "Unpacked in 21270 ms"). At the 2026-08-20 band Step-3 run start the same check pattern returned 0 again and a fetch ran ("Unpacked in 22688 ms"), but a later audit found this was a **wrong-path artifact**: the oleans live at `.lake/packages/mathlib/.lake/build/lib/Mathlib/...` with **no `lean/` path level** (consistent with the case-insensitivity note below), so `ls .../build/lib/lean/Mathlib | wc -l` returns 0 even with the full set present — the correct presence check is a `find … -name "*.olean" | wc -l` at the package build root (5387 with the set intact). A `lake build` invocation killed at its 300 s tool timeout during Mathlib trace replay left the set intact (verified by the corrected count), and one redundant re-fetch ran (idempotent, "Unpacked in 2022 ms"); the final full build of the run was executed detached per the recorded procedure. Recorded operational rule (amended 2026-08-20): check olean presence at the correct path before fetching, never let `lake build` be killed mid-replay — schedule a timeout comfortably above the ~35–50 min residue replay, or elaborate modules directly with `lake env lean`; alternatively run the build detached from the tool timeout (a log file + process poll), which is how the 2026-08-20 Tikhonov run completed its 2185-target build after one invocation was killed at its tool timeout (the Mathlib olean set survived that kill; no re-fetch was needed). Invocation note from the Step-2 run: under `lake --dir=.lake/packages/mathlib`, the `--run` file argument resolves relative to the *current* directory, not `--dir` — invoke from the repo root as `lake --dir=.lake/packages/mathlib env lean --run .lake/packages/mathlib/Cache/Main.lean get` (passing `Cache/Main.lean` alone fails with "file not found"). A macOS case-insensitivity trap was also recorded: creating `.lake/build/lib/lean/` in this workspace shadows the toolchain's core `Lean/` tree in `LEAN_PATH` (breaking all elaboration with "object file ... Lean.Elab.Command.olean does not exist"); this repo's olean layout is `.lake/build/lib/Scaffold/...` directly, with no `lean/` level. Environment healthy at the 2026-08-21 Step-4 run start (correct-path check: 5387 present, no fetch needed); the QA-module batch re-run needed one restart at its 10-minute tool timeout mid-batch (no state damage — `lake env lean` does not replay traces), and completed on the second pass; the full build ran detached per the recorded procedure and passed. |

## Interpretation

- **Fiedler-line stability delivered — the proposal's payoff slice: the Fiedler vector's own rotation, isolated by the common-kernel projector identification (2026-08-28, `proposals/fiedler-subspace-stability-davis-kahan.md` Step 2; zero new axioms, count stays 10; QA +17, total 2731).** The Step-1 statement bounded the rank-2 subspace *containing* the Fiedler cluster; this delivery attributes that rotation to the Fiedler component itself, because connected graphs provably never move their kernel direction — `initialProjector_laplacian_zero_eq_of_connected` makes the constant-mode projector the *same matrix* for any two connected Laplacians, and the residual telescopes to the bounded projector difference. The genuinely new machine is load-bearing on the kernel-characterization ecosystem in both directions: the fixed-space iff is proved *without* connectivity (eigenbasis expansion, bottom-eigenvalue pin, positive-eigenvalue coordinate vanishing), and the identification then runs through `laplacian_mulVec_eq_zero_iff_exists_const` — an error in the kernel characterization, the projector definition, or the eigenbasis machinery breaks exactly this chain rather than passing beside it. The QA's falsification content is three-layered: the headline instance's separation `δ = 2` is discharged against the two exact spectrum pins (only `3 − 1` admits it); the perturbation norm is pinned `= 2` from *both sides* (a wrong constant anywhere in the rank-one/norm→form chain breaks exactly one side); and the disconnected fence *refutes* the identification on the empty graph (`e₀` fixed by the zero Laplacian's projector, not by P₃'s) — the connectivity hypotheses are proved load-bearing, not decorative, with both fix-iff directions exercised on the way. The delivery is unconditional hard crust throughout (the whole chain consumes only proved theorems).
- **Fiedler-subspace stability delivered — `davis_kahan_sin_theta`'s first graph-theoretic consumer, closing the perturbation family's zero-consumer valley at the SGT center (2026-08-28, `proposals/fiedler-subspace-stability-davis-kahan.md` Step 0 verdict + Step 1; zero new axioms, count stays 10; QA +19, total 2714).** The theorem — proved (retired from axiom 2026-08-21) but until now never invoked at a graph object (the derived layer's `davisKahanTwoPoint` consumes it only at abstract matrices; `measure_load_bearing.py` counted zero Mathlib-layer consumers) — is now load-bearing in `fiedlerSubspace_stability`: a wrong index convention, a wrong operator in the separation, or wrong Laplacian arithmetic breaks exactly that statement, and the QA pins the spectra that would catch it. Step 0's verdicts are recorded in the proposal: the "zero consumers anywhere" premise corrected (the derived-layer consumer exists; the true gap was the Mathlib-layer/graph-theoretic one), the delivered Band Davis–Kahan family does NOT subsume Step 1 (two-sided windows vs the one-sided bottom-2 projector — the band route needs strictly more plumbing and would bypass the target theorem), and Step 2's residual-projector route is priced (two of three pieces cheap — the subprojector law IS `spectralProjector_mul_spectralProjector`, the telescoping is immediate — with the common-kernel identification `initialProjector L 0 = initialProjector L' 0` as its own deferred slice). Everything here is unconditional (the wrapper consumes a proved theorem); the QA is two-route and boundary-aware: the P₃ spectrum pinned exact (λ₂ = 1 with the `≥` side new by a sum-of-squares identity on the zero-sum constraint; λ₃ = 3 by trace), the zero-perturbation instance cross-checked against the raw matrix identity, and the K₃ tie-witness proving the hypothesis set EMPTY at the λ₂ = λ₃ = 3 tie — tie-awareness inherited, not silently bounded. **Step 2 has since been delivered** (see the bullet above): the priced common-kernel identification landed as `initialProjector_laplacian_zero_eq_of_connected` and the residual statement as `fiedlerLine_stability`.
- **The empirical stationary distribution delivered — `hoeffding_empirical`'s first theorem consumer, the concentration-axiom family's last zero-consumer scalar member given a real graph-theoretic consumer (2026-08-27, `proposals/empirical-stationary-distribution-concentration.md` Steps 0+1; zero new axioms, count stays 10; QA +29, total 2695).** The axiom's exact clause structure (measurability of `[0,1]`-valued coordinate indicators, their pairwise independence, and the internal centering constant the empirical form carries in its conclusion) is now exercised by a real sampling design where every clause was discharged by a proof — a misstated clause would break the assembly, not merely a vacuous zero-sequence instantiation. The survey verdict that gated the run is recorded in the proposal: no V-valued i.i.d. measure existed in the shelf, and the minimal one is the BernoulliProduct construction at an arbitrary normalized factor — the arithmetic core generalizing verbatim, with the σ-algebra on `V` honestly carried as instance hypotheses. The two Derived theorems are conditional on the axiom alone and say so; the sampling module and the walk-law nonnegativity are pure hard crust. QA is falsification-oriented and two-route throughout: the fixture's joint masses, marginals, and the independence split are each pinned raw against the ∑-∏ machinery; the `n = 0` junk boundary is identified (event `univ`, bound `2`) rather than waved at, with the clean form's `n ≠ 0` hypothesis proved load-bearing (the centering collapse refuted at `0 ≠ 2/3`); and the raw two-sample measure `1/9` meets the axiom-backed bound `2 exp(−1)` at a number proved independently from `Real.add_one_le_exp`. The stationarity-limit form (concentration around `stationaryVec` with the mixing decay folded in as a bias term) is the proposal's priced deferred Step 2, not claimed here.

- **The Ramanujan expansion ceiling delivered — `AlonBoppana.lean`'s first theorem consumer, the Alon–Boppana obstruction joined to the Cheeger lower bound in the field's textbook sentence (2026-08-27, `proposals/ramanujan-expansion-ceiling.md`; zero new axioms, count stays 10; QA +7, total 2666).** The shelf now states, not merely implies, that no d-regular graph satisfying Alon–Boppana's hypotheses can have conductance above `√(2 (1 − 2√(d−1)/d + 2√(d−1)/(d (k+1))))` — the first statement connecting the "how good can expansion be" upper-limit direction to the delivered "how good is this graph's expansion" machinery. Every link is proved hard crust: the ceiling consumes two *theorems* (`alonBoppana_nilli_classical`, `cheeger_lower_bound` — both retired from axiom status in earlier repairs), so the composition is unconditional, and the new engine (`secondEval_smul_of_pos`, `secondEval_congr`) is itself unconditional. The scaling lemma is falsified-against, not merely instantiated: the K₂ two-route pin (`6` by raw trace/determinant/sortedness vs the lemma joined to the pinned `λ₂ = 2`) breaks exactly one route under any wrong constant, and the negative-`c` fence refutes the conclusion where `0 < c` fails (sorted spectrum `[-2, 0]`, second entry `0 ≠ -2`). Scope honesty is a hard acceptance-bar item: nothing in the delivery claims the ceiling is tight or attained — that is the separate Ramanujan-graph construction program; the QA pins non-vacuity as strict slack (`1/4 < √2` on C₈), never tightness.

- **Sparsification Step 1 Slice 3 delivered — the assembly: `matrix_bernstein`'s first real theorem consumer, the program's strategic aim met (2026-08-27, `proposals/spectral-sparsification-via-leverage-scores.md`; zero new axioms, count stays 10; QA +27, total 2659).** The axiom is now *load-bearing in the strategy's sense*: its exact hypothesis shape (strong measurability, pairwise independence, Hermitianity, centering, uniform boundedness, and the variance statistic's Bochner-integral form) is exercised by a real design where every clause was discharged by a proof — a misstated clause would break the assembly, not merely a vacuous instantiation. The deterministic core is exact, not a.s.: the sampled operator carries the saturation guard as a deterministic weight, so `ssSampled ω − Π = ∑ X_e ω` holds at every outcome. The tail constants are the classical ones, *proved* in Slice 2 rather than asserted; the only admitted content is Tropp's inequality itself, reported by `#print axioms` on exactly the two Derived tails and their two QA interface pins. QA is falsification-oriented: the deviation norm is pinned at `1 = 1/q` tight both sides (a wrong constant anywhere in the Slice-2 chain breaks the equality), the transfer lemma's bound is attained with equality at the fixture, the disconnected fence proves connectivity load-bearing precisely at the Foster budget while the tail theorem itself remains connectivity-free (both directions on one fixture), and the event-side witness shows the bound is about a nonempty event. The multiplicative `(1±ε)` refinement on `im Π`-coordinate vectors and the `q ~ log n/ε²` budget corollary remain priced follow-ons, recorded in the proposal.

- **Sparsification Step 1 Slice 2 delivered — the deterministic SS algebra: the `matrix_bernstein` assembly's every input is now a shelf fact at the classical constants (2026-08-27, `proposals/spectral-sparsification-via-leverage-scores.md`; zero new axioms, count stays 10; QA +23, total 2632).** The Step-0 survey's named residual risk — the constants `R = 1/q` and `‖Σ‖ ≤ 1/q`, asserted from the classical argument — is retired by proof: the rank-one bound `‖v vᵀ‖ ≤ v ⬝ᵥ v` (per-eigenvalue Rayleigh + squared C–S in plain dot-product form), the ordered-pair-halved eigen-coordinate edge vectors with `‖v_e‖² = w_e R_eff/2` (the Foster spectral machinery's own representation — no pseudoinverse, no matrix square root), the projector identity `∑_e v_e v_eᵀ = Π_{im L}` exactly (the halving absorbing the ordered-pair double count so the sampling space is the delivered `bernPMF` at `ι = V × V` verbatim), and the three `matrix_bernstein` clause lemmas at the guarded design: `∫ X_e = 0` with *no connectivity hypothesis* (zero-leverage pairs absorbed by the zero rank-one factor — the junk `1/0 = 0` lands on the right value), the uniform `‖X_e ω‖ ≤ 1/q` (the Finding-A guard load-bearing: the *unguarded* saturated summand is refuted in QA at `1/4 < 1/2`), and `‖Σ‖ ≤ 1/q` (coefficients dominated, the residual rank-one sum *exactly* the projector, the projector's Rayleigh form dominated by `x ⬝ᵥ x`). QA is falsification-oriented: the budget pin runs two independent routes (Foster's corollary vs the raw four-pair enumeration), the rank-one norm identity is pinned two-sided at a concrete vector, the variance coefficient is exact (`1/2` at `p = 1/2`), and the two fences prove the budget hypothesis `q > 0` and the guard each load-bearing by refuting the conclusion at the junk values.

- **Alon–Boppana Step 5 delivered — the program COMPLETE: the diameter-dependent single-graph statement is on the shelf as pure hard crust (2026-08-27, `proposals/alon-boppana-bound.md`; zero new axioms, count stays 10; QA +9, total 2585).** `alonBoppana_nilli` is the program's capstone: `secondEval (d•1 − A) ≤ d − (1 + 2k√(d−1))/(k+1)` — Alon–Boppana's `2√(d−1)` barrier with the error term explicit — under exactly the two-edge hypotheses Steps 2–4 built (tree balls at radius `k+1`, far-apart at threshold `2(k+1)+1`), never derived from the diameter (the qualification trap the proposal documents; `alonBoppana_diam_ge` states the honest hypothesis-side constraint `k+1 ≤ ⌊diam/2⌋` instead). The bridge `distEdge_le_diam` retired the Step-2 priced blocker (`ediam ≠ ⊤` on finite connected input). The two residual QA witnesses are falsification-oriented in the strategy's sense: the loop-pair fence proves `IsTreeBall` load-bearing exactly where the equal-mass lemma consumes it (a loop's level-0 class has one element, and the two-vector orthogonality genuinely fails at mass `1`), and the integer-witness route reaches the same conclusion as the theorem stack — strictly better, at `2/3` vs `1` — through a structurally different vector and fully raw arithmetic (three independent numbers, no theorem input), so a wrong `secondEval_le_rayleigh` engine, PSD supplier, or kernel fact would break exactly that route.

- **Alon–Boppana Step 3's second sub-slice delivered — the energy half: Nilli's numerator bounded from below on the existing tree-ball predicate (2026-08-27, `proposals/alon-boppana-bound.md`; zero new axioms, count stays 10; QA +11, total 2560).** The recorded hard piece — cardinality equations give level sizes, not level-to-level edge counts — is resolved by harvesting parent edges *from below*: every level-`j ≥ 1` vertex has one (the parent lemma, itself junk-safe and connectivity-free), and a lower bound on the numerator is all the variational route needs since the denominator is exact. `radialVec_quadForm_ge` is load-bearing in the strategy's exact sense: its per-level collapse consumes `IsTreeBall`'s equations, `levE`'s Lipschitz behavior, and `radialVec`'s shape together, and the C₈ pin is *tight* — every harvested term is visible in the raw number (the edge's `2` plus the four level-1 parent edges' `4`). The Rayleigh corollary is the exact interface Step 4's orthogonalization consumes.

- **Alon–Boppana Step 3's first sub-slice delivered — the radial test vector exists on the shelf with its normalization computed exactly (2026-08-27, `proposals/alon-boppana-bound.md`; zero new axioms, count stays 10; QA +10, total 2549).** The proposal priced Step 3 as the crux needing sub-decomposition; the first slice is the denominator half of Nilli's Rayleigh quotient. `radialVec_dotProduct_self` is load-bearing growth in the strategy's exact sense: its statement would fail to typecheck if `IsTreeBall`'s level equations, `ballE`'s filter shape, or `levE`'s definition were wrong (each enters the per-level collapse `2 (d−1)^j · ρ^{2j} = 2`), and the QA pins it against an independent raw route that shares no mechanism with the theorem — a wrong level count, level power, or normalization constant breaks exactly one of the two routes at the same number. The `d = 1` fence proves the module's own docstring claim materially: the normalization hypothesis is junk-satisfiable at `ρ = 0` (`0⁻¹ = 0` in ℝ) while the truncated `(d−1)^j` kills the level counts, and the identity then genuinely fails (`2 ≠ 4`) — `1 < d` is load-bearing at precisely the `mul_inv_cancel₀` step, fenced before the energy half (Step 3b) divides by this norm.

- **Alon–Boppana Step 2 delivered — the tree-ball interface the remaining steps stand on, with the qualification trap's negative witness already in place (2026-08-26, `proposals/alon-boppana-bound.md`; zero new axioms, count stays 10; QA +19, total 2539).** The Step-0 verdict is now shelf fact: "the radius-r ball around the edge is a tree" enters the library as **cardinality equations** (`IsTreeBall`: level `j` carries exactly `2 (d−1)^j` vertices), never a structural tree-ness predicate, and every distance cost the spike priced is amortized behind four interface lemmas — the junk-zero-honest level-0 iff (the `dist` trap stated, not hidden), its connected form, the endpoint-pair level-0 class, and ball membership. QA is falsification-oriented at three levels: the C₈ **positive** exercises the full hypothesis set at the Step-0 verdict's exact fixture (the antipodal edge pair, levels full at `k = 2`, balls disjoint at cross distances ≥ 3 proved by *short-walk exhaustion* — no walk witnesses needed since only lower bounds ever enter — and the layer-cake cardinality by two independent routes, set enumeration vs the theorem); the C₄ **wrap-around negative** refutes `IsTreeBall` at radius 3 (level 2 demanded at two vertices and provably empty — the fullness equations carry real geometric content, failing exactly when the BFS ball wraps a short diameter; this is the qualification trap's QA witness, present *before* the theorem exists); and the **threshold-tightness fence** proves the strict inequality of the far-apart condition necessary (near-antipodal balls at min cross-distance exactly `r + s` share vertex `2`).

- **The Alon–Boppana program opened at its adopted gate — the lower companion to the Cheeger/expander toolkit begins with the d-regularity interface, priced tree-ball first (2026-08-26, `proposals/alon-boppana-bound.md` ADOPTED by operator decision this day; zero new axioms, count stays 10; QA +14, total 2520, `AlonBoppana_QA` a new file).** Until this delivery the shelf held the entire "small spectral gap ⇒ good expansion" direction without any statement of the matching lower bound on how small a d-regular gap can be; the adopted Route A (Nilli's variational method) needs exactly three interfaces, and Step 1 delivers the first: `IsDRegular` in the shelf's `d : ℝ` hypothesis idiom, the constant-eigenvector fact, the AM–GM row-sum domination, and the **top-eigenvalue identification `evals ⟨last⟩ = d`** — load-bearing on the sorted-spectrum API at its extremes because it needs *both* halves (the eigenvalue witness at `onesVec` and the domination at the unit eigenvector; a wrong `evals`/`eigvalOf`/`deg` shape breaks one of them), which QA pins at two raw-computed fixtures. - **The signed-graph slice delivered — the graph-model axis's named absent category gains its first occupant, and the magnetic program carries weight from a second theorem family (2026-08-26, `proposals/signed-graphs-balance.md` COMPLETE; zero new axioms, count stays 10; QA +62, total 2506, `Signed_QA` a new file).** The load-bearing-growth principle executed on the 2026-08-25 magnetic delivery: a `{±1}` signing is exactly a magnetic potential at the π-flux, so the signed Dirichlet energy identity is *derived from* `magnetic_energy` (a wrong magnetic identity breaks the signed theorems — witnessed numerically by the complex energy pin at `![1, I, 1]`), and the form-level gauge characterization is promoted to the kernel at action level in `Magnetic.lean` itself (`magneticLaplacian_mulVec_eq_zero_iff` — pure row algebra at unit modulus, the module's own strengthening). On that bridge stands the **Harary balance theorem in kernel form** — `IsBalanced A s ↔ ∃ x ≠ 0, L_σ *ᵥ x = 0` on connected symmetric nonnegative input — with the frustrated side positive definite, and the **switching similarity** `diag(g) · L_σ · diag(g) = laplacian A` with two-way eigenpair transfer (the classical "balanced ⟹ spectrally unsigned" fact). - **Explicit axiom:** an intentional trust boundary declared with Lean's `axiom` command. QA lemmas and derived theorems using these axioms are conditional on them.
- **Admitted proof:** a `sorry` or `admit` accepted by Lean; this is different from an explicit axiom and remains technical debt in public modules. As of this scoreboard there are no `sorry`/`admit` tokens anywhere under `Scaffold/`.
- **QA declaration:** a theorem or lemma under `Scaffold/QA`; all 1243 declarations have compiled against the current public API.
- **Default build:** the umbrella reaches every public module listed above; no public module is excluded from `lake build`.
- **Decidable spectral certificates, step 2 — the Expander Mixing Lemma (2026-08-19):** `proposals/decidable-spectral-certificates.md` (High) at its named next step, delivered as pure hard crust in `GraphTheory.Expander` plus two generic additions to `GraphTheory.Spectral` — **zero new axioms** (13 unchanged). **The theorem:** for a symmetric, nonnegative, `d`-regular network with Laplacian-spectrum hypothesis `μ ≥ max |d − λ₂(L)| |d − λ_max(L)|`, the `(S,T)` cut weight deviates from the population main term `d•|S|•|T|/|V|` by at most `μ • √(|S||T|(|V|−|S|)(|V|−|T|))/|V|` — the classical discrepancy bridge between algebraic spectral gaps and combinatorial pseudorandomness, at the Step 0 restated `√` signature. **The route is cheaper and stronger than the sketched eigenspace expansion:** a Rayleigh sandwich on `1⊥` — the `d`-regular identity `A + L = D` in quadratic-form form trading the proved lower bound `λ₂•‖x‖² ≤ xᵀLx` (the center's `secondEval_le_rayleigh` multiplied out) against the new generic top bound `xᵀLx ≤ λ_max•‖x‖²` (`quadForm_le_evals_last`, supported by `eigvalOf_le_evals_last`) — giving the operator bound `|xᵀAx| ≤ μ‖x‖²` on `1⊥`, then the sharp bilinear form `(x ⬝ᵥ (A *ᵥ y))² ≤ μ²‖x‖²‖y‖²` by the scaling trick (`√Y•x ± √X•y` through the polarization identity; the choice `a² = Y, b² = X` attains the AM–GM equality — no slack), then the variance identity `‖centeredIndicator S‖² = |S|(|V|−|S|)/|V|` evaluating the geometric factor. No eigenspace split of `A`, no kernel case analysis, no affine spectrum transfer. **Statement-shape strengthening recorded:** the Step 0 sketch's `hloop` hypothesis is dropped — the `A + L = D` identity needs regularity only. The specialization is load-bearing on the Woodbury repair — a residual defect there would surface in this proof rather than pass beside it. QA (`Core/MatrixUpdates_QA.lean`, +4 declarations): the rank-one positive instance at the existing `A2 = diag 2 2` fixture — `A + u vᵀ = !![3,1;1,3]` — with the left side computed independently by the adjugate formula (`sherman_morrison_sum_inv_QA`) and the theorem-consumed right side pinned to the same `!![3/8,-1/8;-1/8,3/8]` (`sherman_morrison_rhs_QA`, denominator computed to `1` from the definitions); and the negative witness at the excluded denominator — `A = !![1]`, `u = ![2]`, `v = ![-1/2]` gives `v ⬝ᵥ (A⁻¹ *ᵥ u) = -1` exactly (`sherman_morrison_excluded_denominator_QA`) and the update `!![1] + !![-1] = !![0]` is singular there (`sherman_morrison_singular_witness_QA`) — the hypothesis excludes exactly the vacuous-junk point.

It is now a theorem at the same name with the standard middle factor `(C⁻¹ + V A⁻¹ U)⁻¹` and exactly the three explicit hypotheses `IsUnit A.det`, `IsUnit C.det`, `IsUnit (C⁻¹ + V A⁻¹ U).det` (the former `IsUnit (A + U C V).det` hypothesis is derivable and dropped — Mathlib's `Matrix.invertibleAddMulMul` constructs the sum's `Invertible` instance). - **`lambda2_variational` retirement (2026-08-18):** the Courant–Fischer characterization of the algebraic connectivity was converted from an admitted axiom (symmetry hypotheses only — a materially false shape for negative weights, refuted in QA) into a proved theorem with the load-bearing `hnonneg` hypothesis, reducing the explicit axiom count from 18 to 17. The proof is pure matrix-world hard crust (eigenbasis expansion, Parseval, spectral resolution of `quadForm`, and two sorted-multiset multiplicity pins), consuming no axioms.
- **Azuma statement repair (2026-08-17, same day as the axiom's introduction):** preparing the derivation exposed that `matrix_azuma_hoeffding` lacked the summand-count factor `m` in the exponent denominator, which made the statement false for `m ≥ 2` (Rademacher-sum counterexample). The denominator is now `8 m R²`, the uniform-bound specialization of Tropp's variance statistic `σ² = ‖∑ Yₖ²‖ ≤ m R²`. The axiom had no downstream consumers before the repair.
